Title: Sumario, y memorial ajustado de las probanzas, que por deposiciones de testigos, e instrumentos se an hecho por el illustrissimo señor D. Pedro de Villagomez, arzobispo de los Reyes, y demas señores jueces apostolicos, de la beatificacion, y canonizacion del siervo de Dios el illustrissimo señor don Toribio Alfonso Mogrovejo, arzobispo que fue desta ciudad
Creator: Pedro de Villagómez (1589-1671), archbishop of Lima, and the apostolic judges of the cause
Publisher: Lima, 1662 (the printer's name is garbled in the scan)
Publication Date: 1662
Rights: Public Domain (published 1662, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: spa
Source: Internet Archive item sumarioymemorial00vill (https://archive.org/details/sumarioymemorial00vill), the item's own sumarioymemorial00vill_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: John Carter Brown Library; host metadata date: 1662. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: dec84ccb91fe75fabbf00a735d0e96eb5bd0935a6bda75125eaaff7b1724cba4.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. John Carter Brown Library scan; usable OCR with the long s read as f; the title page is garbled in places and the printed marginal summaries are interleaved.
Content note: The digest of the witness depositions in the Lima process for the beatification of Archbishop Toribio de Mogrovejo (died 1606): article by article, each witness named with his rank and state and his testimony summarised in the third person, on the archbishop's life, virtues, visitations and miracles.
